# All Supabase SQL

Every SQL statement this project **ships** to Supabase, in the order it was
created: the versioned migration set, plus the applier's own bootstrap. One
section per file in `supabase/migrations/`, grouped by the app version that was
current when the phase landed; the text under each label is that migration file
**verbatim**. Two things are deliberately not here, and neither changes the
server: the assertions in `supabase/tests/*.sql` (runnable checks that roll back)
and the read-only counts `tool/check_supabase_seed.dart` and
`tool/apply_supabase_migrations.dart` issue.

- Only one project is ever in scope: `lsthasywrdrhuuxnykau` ("Legal Case Diary").
  AGENTS.md rule 12 names the live project that must never be touched.
- Server SQL is written only as a migration file, then this ledger is rebuilt.
  Nothing here is typed by hand, so the two can never disagree.
- Re-running the whole set is safe: creates are `if not exists`, functions are
  `create or replace`, every policy and trigger is preceded by a `drop ... if
  exists`, and all six seed inserts upsert `where <table>.is_user_modified = false`.
- One setting is dashboard-only and is not SQL: Realtime Authorization
  (`supabase/README.md`). pg_cron is installed and the tombstone purge
  scheduled by the last migration — verified on the project.
- Every version here is recorded in `supabase_migrations.schema_migrations` on
  `lsthasywrdrhuuxnykau`, so `supabase db push` sees the set as applied.

| 0.0.325 | 2026-09-30 | `20260930190000_remove_firm_member.sql` | Task 20 Phase 2's `remove_firm_member(uuid)`: owner-only, `SECURITY DEFINER`, it deletes the membership row and relinks the member's `profiles.firm_id` / `role` to individual — which is what revokes RLS access, Realtime topic authorization and every queued write at once, without touching the auth session (this project has no service-role key). Authored records keep their attribution and are never reassigned; the removal is announced to the removed account's own topic and to the firm's. The same migration attaches the existing broadcast trigger to `firm_members`, so a permission toggle reaches the member it affects. |
| 0.0.327 | 2026-09-30 | `20260930200000_push_changes_delete_review.sql` | Task 20 Phase 4's delete-review branch: `push_changes` replaces whole to teach an approval (a `delete_requests` update setting `status = 'approved'`) what it means server-side. The request is locked (already) and the target is locked next, always in that order so concurrent approvals cannot deadlock; only a member with `can_delete` may review; a target at a different version than the request's `target_base_version` becomes `target_conflict`, parking the request as `stale_review_required` with the version the reviewer just saw so a reconfirm re-decides against the row that exists; an already soft-deleted target becomes `already_resolved` with its tombstone and records no decision; and an unchanged target is tombstoned atomically with its `case_history` cascade and the request resolved beside it, all inside the per-mutation savepoint that already produces idempotent receipts. |
| 0.0.330 | 2026-09-30 | `20260930210000_sync_can_write_firm_membership.sql` | Task 20 audit closure: `sync_can_write` again, so authorship of a firm-scoped row only counts while the row's firm is still the caller's firm (`firm_id = current_firm_id()`). Removing an associate relinks `profiles.firm_id` to NULL, but the author clause checked `user_id = auth.uid()` before any firm helper, so a removed member could still merge a queued **update** to a firm record they happened to author. The narrowing is applied to the same three places that trusted authorship: ordinary rows, custom references, and a requester's own pending delete request. A `firm_id`-less personal row is unaffected, and `can_edit_firm` / `can_delete_firm` still cover teammates and reviewers. |
| 0.0.332 | 2026-09-30 | `20260930220000_rls_firm_membership.sql` | Task 20 audit follow-up: the same membership rule, applied to RLS. `sync_can_write` closed the *app* path in 0.0.330, but the table policies still said `user_id = auth.uid()` on a firm-scoped row, so a removed associate could still write their authored firm rows through PostgREST directly — rewriting one, or detaching it from the firm by nulling `firm_id`. Every INSERT and UPDATE policy that trusts authorship now requires the row to be personal (`firm_id is null`) or still the caller's firm (`is not distinct from current_firm_id()`), across the ten tables that share one shape plus `conflict_log` and the requester's own pending `delete_requests` row. SELECT policies and table privileges are deliberately untouched. |

## Bootstrap — Applier Bookkeeping (before the first apply)

> **Created before the first apply.** Applies to every run of `tool/apply_supabase_migrations.dart`: it creates the migration-history objects once with the Supabase CLI's own DDL, then records one row per applied file through the last statement below. Everything is `if not exists` or `on conflict`, so a project the CLI already linked is left untouched.

```sql
-- Create the migration-history schema.
CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS supabase_migrations;
-- Create the migration-history table.
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS supabase_migrations.schema_migrations (version text NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY);
-- Add the statements column (absent on projects whose history predates the CLI column).
ALTER TABLE supabase_migrations.schema_migrations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS statements text[];
-- Add the name column.
ALTER TABLE supabase_migrations.schema_migrations ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS name text;

-- Run once per migration file, inside that file's transaction,
-- so a file is either fully applied and recorded, or neither.
INSERT INTO supabase_migrations.schema_migrations(version, name, statements) VALUES ('<version>', '<name>', NULL) ON CONFLICT (version) DO UPDATE SET name = EXCLUDED.name, statements = EXCLUDED.statements;
```

## App Version 0.0.289 — Task 16 Phase 1 — Core Schema, Sign-Up Trigger And Reference Seed

### 0.0.289 · `20260929100000_phase1_core_schema.sql`

> **Created 2026-09-29.** Creates every Supabase table at once — 12 push/pull domain, 3 pull-only cache, 2 per-user preference and 2 server-managed — mirroring the shipped Drift columns. RLS is switched on in the same statement that creates each table.

```sql
-- Phase 1 (1/2) — Core schema: every Supabase table, created up front so later
-- phases only add behaviour. Column names/types mirror the shipped Drift
-- tables one-for-one (Task 03) so push/pull is lossless; the global sync
-- columns are added only to the push/pull domain class.
--
-- Table classes (AGENTS.md rule 4):
--   push/pull domain  : cases, case_history, notes, case_references,
--                       court_categories, case_categories, courts, steps,
--                       case_types, on_behalfs, delete_requests, conflict_log
--   pull-only cache   : profiles, firms, firm_members
--   per-user pref     : user_preferences, case_notification_preferences
--   server-managed    : firm_invites, payment_submissions
--   (payment_approval_events, mutation_receipts, invite_attempts and app_admins
--    are created in Phase 2.)
--
-- Type conventions (implementation_plan.md, "Timestamp Convention" + Revision
-- 2.4): client display metadata stays BIGINT epoch milliseconds — epoch ms does
-- not fit in a 32-bit INTEGER — while server-authoritative instants are
-- TIMESTAMPTZ. Row keys the client generates (cases, notes, …) are TEXT so a
-- client-supplied UUID string is never rejected; identity/scope columns
-- (user_id, firm_id, approved_by, …) are UUID with FKs to auth.users/firms.
-- Reference-data keys are the stable deterministic seed tags shared by Drift,
-- Supabase and backups (Rev 2.4 §1); they stay TEXT.

-- pgcrypto supplies gen_random_uuid() and, through public.sha256_hex() in
-- Phase 3, the SHA-256 hashing join_firm() needs.
create extension if not exists pgcrypto with schema extensions;

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Account & firm scope (pull-only caches on the client)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Firms first: profiles.firm_id and firm_members.firm_id both reference it.
create table if not exists public.firms (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  name text not null,
  owner_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  subscription_status text not null default 'free',
  subscription_expiry timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
comment on table public.firms is
  'Pull-only cache. subscription_expiry is written only by mirror_owner_subscription_to_firm() (Phase 3).';

-- One row per authenticated user; created by the sign-up trigger (part 2).
create table if not exists public.profiles (
  id uuid primary key references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  full_name text not null default '',
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  role text not null default 'individual',
  subscription_status text not null default 'free',
  subscription_expiry timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now()
);
comment on table public.profiles is
  'Pull-only cache. subscription_expiry is the single source of truth for premium entitlement; subscription_status is display-only.';

create table if not exists public.firm_members (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  firm_id uuid not null references public.firms (id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  role text not null,
  can_add boolean not null default false,
  can_edit boolean not null default false,
  can_delete boolean not null default false,
  synced_at bigint,
  permissions_synced_at bigint,
  unique (firm_id, user_id)
);
comment on table public.firm_members is
  'Pull-only cache of membership and permission flags; the authoritative permission source for RLS and triggers.';

-- Single-use onboarding codes. Redeemed only inside join_firm() (Phase 3).
create table if not exists public.firm_invites (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  firm_id uuid not null references public.firms (id) on delete cascade,
  code_hash text not null unique,
  created_by uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  used_by uuid references auth.users (id),
  used_at bigint,
  revoked boolean not null default false,
  created_at bigint not null
);
comment on table public.firm_invites is
  'Plaintext codes are never stored; code_hash is sha256 hex of the normalized code (see public.sha256_hex).';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Push/pull domain: editable reference data (stable deterministic TEXT keys)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Global sync columns for every domain table:
--   updated_at       bigint      client edit time, never used for authorization
--   version          integer     server-assigned revision (1 on insert)
--   server_updated_at timestamptz trigger-maintained; the only delta-pull cursor
--   synced_at        bigint      device mirror metadata
--   is_deleted       boolean     soft delete; rows are never hard-deleted
--   last_modified_by text        server-assigned editor id

create table if not exists public.court_categories (
  id text primary key,
  name_key text not null,
  court_level text not null,
  order_index integer not null,
  division text,
  district text,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);
comment on table public.court_categories is
  'Stable machine keys (appellate_division, …). Built-ins are global; custom rows are user- or firm-scoped.';

create table if not exists public.case_categories (
  id text primary key,
  court_category_id text not null references public.court_categories (id),
  name text not null,
  order_index integer not null,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

create table if not exists public.courts (
  id text primary key,
  court_name text not null,
  higher_lower_courts text not null,
  division text,
  district text,
  court_category_id text not null references public.court_categories (id),
  order_index integer not null default 0,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

create table if not exists public.steps (
  id text primary key,
  court_category_id text not null references public.court_categories (id),
  step_name text not null,
  order_index integer not null,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

create table if not exists public.case_types (
  id text primary key,
  court_category_id text not null references public.court_categories (id),
  case_category_id text references public.case_categories (id),
  name text not null,
  order_index integer not null,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

create table if not exists public.on_behalfs (
  id text primary key,
  court_category_id text not null references public.court_categories (id),
  name text not null,
  order_index integer not null,
  is_custom boolean not null default false,
  is_user_modified boolean not null default false,
  user_id uuid references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Push/pull domain: case data
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table if not exists public.cases (
  id text primary key,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  court_id text not null references public.courts (id),
  case_type text not null,
  case_category_id text references public.case_categories (id),
  case_number text not null,
  honorable_judges text,
  tender_number text,
  previous_court_case text,
  district_of_origin text,
  petitioner text not null,
  petitioner_lead_counsel text,
  respondent text not null,
  respondent_lead_counsel text,
  on_behalf_id text not null references public.on_behalfs (id),
  party_phone text,
  case_title_and_section text,
  case_remarks text,
  next_date bigint,
  last_step text,
  notes_results text,
  result text,
  created_at bigint not null,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);
comment on table public.cases is
  'Mirrors the shipped client model, whose only phone column is party_phone: the represented client''s number (Rev 2.4''s separate petitioner_phone/respondent_phone split was withdrawn by user decision on 2026-09-29, not deferred - do not add those columns).';

-- Immutable hearing timeline. source_mutation_id is the idempotency key of the
-- accepted mutation that created the row; retries cannot duplicate it.
create table if not exists public.case_history (
  id text primary key,
  case_id text not null references public.cases (id) on delete cascade,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  hearing_date bigint not null,
  last_step text,
  next_date bigint,
  note_result text,
  result text,
  created_by uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  source_mutation_id text not null unique,
  base_case_version integer not null,
  result_case_version integer not null,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

create table if not exists public.notes (
  id text primary key,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  title text not null,
  body text not null,
  items text,
  is_pinned boolean not null default false,
  created_at bigint not null,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);
comment on column public.notes.items is
  'To-do checklist as a JSON array of {text, done}; null on a plain text note.';

create table if not exists public.case_references (
  id text primary key,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  reference_and_page_no text not null,
  statute text,
  section_or_article text,
  title_and_description text,
  comments_notes text,
  is_pinned boolean not null default false,
  created_at bigint not null,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

-- Deletion request queue for associates without can_delete.
create table if not exists public.delete_requests (
  id text primary key,
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  entity_type text not null,
  entity_id text not null,
  entity_snapshot text not null,
  target_base_version integer not null,
  requested_by uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  status text not null,
  reviewed_by uuid references auth.users (id),
  review_note text,
  created_at bigint not null,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);
comment on column public.delete_requests.entity_snapshot is
  'Snapshot of the target at request time, stored as TEXT so a JSON string cannot silently become a JSON string value.';

-- Mirror of the local rejected-mutation log, pulled on a new device for
-- portability. user_id/firm_id are ownership columns the local table does not
-- carry: RLS and the Realtime topic both need them.
create table if not exists public.conflict_log (
  id text primary key,
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  firm_id uuid references public.firms (id),
  mutation_id text not null,
  entity_type text not null,
  entity_id text not null,
  base_version integer not null,
  base_payload text not null,
  submitted_payload text not null,
  changed_fields text not null,
  server_version integer not null,
  server_payload text not null,
  resolution_state text not null,
  conflict_at bigint not null,
  resolved_at bigint,
  is_dismissed boolean not null default false,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at bigint not null,
  version integer not null default 1,
  server_updated_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  synced_at bigint,
  is_deleted boolean not null default false,
  last_modified_by text
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Per-user preferences (never firm-shared)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Server side of the premium cross-device preference sync (Rev 2.4 §4). Rows
-- are self-only under RLS.
create table if not exists public.user_preferences (
  user_id uuid primary key references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  language text,
  theme text,
  view_settings_json text,
  notification_enabled boolean not null default false,
  notification_time bigint,
  notification_default_offsets text,
  updated_at bigint not null
);

create table if not exists public.case_notification_preferences (
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  case_id text not null references public.cases (id) on delete cascade,
  enabled boolean not null default true,
  offsets text,
  time_minutes integer,
  updated_at bigint not null,
  primary key (user_id, case_id)
);

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Server-managed: payment submissions (no Drift counterpart; per the plan)
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

create table if not exists public.payment_submissions (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id uuid not null references auth.users (id),
  plan_type text not null,
  amount integer not null,
  mfs_provider text not null,
  sender_number text not null,
  transaction_id text not null unique,
  status text not null default 'pending',
  admin_notes text,
  created_at bigint not null,
  submitted_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  approved_at timestamptz,
  approved_by uuid references auth.users (id),
  updated_at bigint not null
);
comment on column public.payment_submissions.submitted_at is
  'Authoritative server submission time; the BEFORE INSERT trigger overwrites any client value and the 3-per-24h limit counts it.';

-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-- Row-level security is switched on with the tables, not later
-- ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

-- Supabase grants `anon` and `authenticated` table privileges by default, so a
-- table is only protected once RLS is on: with RLS on and no policy yet, every
-- client command is denied. Enabling it here means a migration run that stops
-- half-way can never leave a reachable table behind; Phase 4 adds the policies
-- (and re-asserts the same statements, which is idempotent).

alter table public.profiles enable row level security;
alter table public.firms enable row level security;
alter table public.firm_members enable row level security;
alter table public.firm_invites enable row level security;
alter table public.court_categories enable row level security;
alter table public.case_categories enable row level security;
alter table public.courts enable row level security;
alter table public.steps enable row level security;
alter table public.case_types enable row level security;
alter table public.on_behalfs enable row level security;
alter table public.cases enable row level security;
alter table public.case_history enable row level security;
alter table public.notes enable row level security;
alter table public.case_references enable row level security;
alter table public.delete_requests enable row level security;
alter table public.conflict_log enable row level security;
alter table public.user_preferences enable row level security;
alter table public.case_notification_preferences enable row level security;
alter table public.payment_submissions enable row level security;
```

### 0.0.289 · `20260929100100_phase1_signup_profile_trigger.sql`

> **Created 2026-09-29.** handle_new_user() and its on_auth_user_created trigger: each new auth user gets an individual, free, firm-less profiles row. The only INSERT path for profiles.

```sql
-- Phase 1 (2/2) — Sign-up trigger: every new auth.users row gets a profiles row.
--
-- The profile starts as an individual account (`role = 'individual'`,
-- `firm_id = null`, free entitlement) exactly as the plan describes. It is a
-- pull-only cache on the client, so this trigger is the only INSERT path.
--
-- EXECUTE is deliberately NOT revoked from PUBLIC here: PostgreSQL checks the
-- EXECUTE privilege of the role whose statement fires a trigger, and sign-up
-- inserts into auth.users run as the auth service role, so revoking would break
-- account creation. Nothing else is reachable through this function.

create or replace function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = pg_catalog, public
as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (
    id, full_name, role, firm_id,
    subscription_status, subscription_expiry, created_at
  )
  values (
    new.id,
    coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'full_name', ''),
    'individual',
    null,
    'free',
    null,
    now()
  )
  on conflict (id) do nothing;

  return new;
end;
$$;

comment on function public.handle_new_user() is
  'Creates the individual profiles row for a new auth user; see Phase 4 for profiles RLS.';

drop trigger if exists on_auth_user_created on auth.users;
create trigger on_auth_user_created
  after insert on auth.users
  for each row
  execute function public.handle_new_user();
```

### 0.0.289 · `20260929100200_phase1_seed_reference_data.sql`

> **Created 2026-09-29.** Generated built-in reference data — 1,442 rows over six editable tables, under the same stable IDs the client seeds — upserted so a customized row is never overwritten.

```sql
-- Phase 1 — Built-in reference data, generated by tool/generate_supabase_seed.dart.
-- Do not edit by hand: change lib/core/constants/pre_seeded_data.dart and re-run
--   dart run tool/generate_supabase_seed.dart
--
-- Every ID here is the stable deterministic tag the Drift seed migrations
-- (Task 04) already use, so a device, the server and a backup all name the same
-- row. Rows are upserted with `on conflict (id) do update ... where
-- is_user_modified = false`, mirroring the client's seed runner: a row the user
-- or firm has customized is never overwritten by a later seed. `version` starts
-- at 1 (the server's insert revision) and re-seeding never bumps it; the
-- `server_updated_at` trigger stamps the pull cursor.
--
-- Table class: all six tables are push/pull domain (editable, firm-shared
-- reference data with user-scoped customizations in individual mode).

insert into public.court_categories (id, name_key, court_level, order_index, division, district, is_custom, is_user_modified, user_id, firm_id, updated_at, version, synced_at, is_deleted, last_modified_by)
values
  ('appellate_division', 'courtCategory_appellateDivision', 'appellate', 0, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('high_court_division', 'courtCategory_highCourtDivision', 'high_court', 1, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('district_sessions_criminal', 'lowerCourtCategory_districtSessionsCriminal', 'lower', 3, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('district_courts_civil', 'lowerCourtCategory_districtCourtsCivil', 'lower', 2, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('metropolitan_sessions_judge_criminal', 'lowerCourtCategory_metropolitanSessionsJudgeCriminal', 'lower', 4, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('chief_judicial_magistrate', 'lowerCourtCategory_chiefJudicialMagistrate', 'lower', 5, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('chief_metropolitan_magistrate', 'lowerCourtCategory_chiefMetropolitanMagistrate', 'lower', 6, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null),
  ('executive_magistrate', 'lowerCourtCategory_executiveMagistrate', 'lower', 7, null, null, false, false, null, null, 1767225600000, 1, null, false, null)
on conflict (id) do update set
  name_key = excluded.name_key,
  court_level = excluded.court_level,
  order_index = excluded.order_index,
  updated_at = excluded.updated_at
where court_categories.is_user_modified = false;

insert into public.case_categories (id, court_category_id, name, order_index, is_custom, is_user_modified, user_id, firm_id, updated_at, version, synced_at, is_deleted, last_modified_by)
```
